-- kaveri.aadhar_authentication_log definition

-- Drop table

-- DROP TABLE kaveri.aadhar_authentication_log;

CREATE TABLE kaveri.aadhar_authentication_log (
	id int8 DEFAULT nextval('kaveri.aadhar_log_id_seq'::regclass) NOT NULL,
	applicationnumber varchar(50) NULL,
	partyid int8 NULL,
	linkpartyid int8 NULL,
	nameasperaadhar varchar(500) NULL,
	masked_aadhar varchar(500) NULL,
	aadharhash varchar(500) NULL,
	vault_id varchar(500) NULL,
	ekyctxnno varchar(500) NULL,
	ekyctimestamp varchar(500) NULL,
	responsestatus varchar(500) NULL,
	errormessage varchar(500) NULL,
	error varchar(500) NULL,
	inserteddatetime timestamp DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP NULL,
	CONSTRAINT pk_aadhar_log_id PRIMARY KEY (id),
	CONSTRAINT uk_aadhar_authentication_log UNIQUE (ekyctxnno)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_authentication_log_appno ON kaveri.aadhar_authentication_log USING btree (applicationnumber);
CREATE INDEX idx_authentication_log_prtyid ON kaveri.aadhar_authentication_log USING btree (partyid);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_aadhar_authentication_log_ccnew ON kaveri.aadhar_authentication_log USING btree (ekyctxnno);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_aadhar_authentication_log_ccnew1 ON kaveri.aadhar_authentication_log USING btree (ekyctxnno);

    or
delete
    or
update
    on
    kaveri.cc_applicationdetails for each row execute function kavericdc.fn_cc_applicationdetails_audit();

CREATE INDEX idx_regionmaster ON kaveri.regionmaster USING btree (regionid, isactive);

insert
    or
delete
    or
update
    on
    kaveri.regionmaster for each row execute function kavericdc.fn_insert_mdm_audit();

CREATE TABLE kaveri.departmentusers_deo_service_history (
	uid int8 DEFAULT nextval('kaveri.departmentusers_deo_serviceid_seq'::regclass) NOT NULL,
	from_userid int8 NOT NULL,
	from_srocode int4 NOT NULL,
	from_designationid int4 NULL,
	from_officeid int2 NULL,
	from_firstname varchar(100) NULL,
	from_lastname varchar(100) NULL,
	from_middlename varchar(100) NULL,
	from_phone varchar(100) NULL,
	to_userid int8 NOT NULL,
	to_srocode int4 NOT NULL,
	to_designationid int4 NULL,
	to_officeid int2 NULL,
	to_firstname varchar(100) NULL,
	to_lastname varchar(100) NULL,
	to_middlename varchar(100) NULL,
	to_phone varchar(100) NULL,
	serviceexpdate timestamp NULL,
	updatedby int4 NULL,
	servicestartdate timestamp NULL,
	employeetypeid int4 NULL,
	istransfer bool NULL,
	k1k2_flag int2 DEFAULT 2 NULL,
	inserteddate timestamp DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP NULL,
	from_districtcode int2 NULL,
	to_districtcode int2 NULL,
	CONSTRAINT departmentusers_deo_service_history_pkey PRIMARY KEY (uid)
);
CREATE INDEX departmentusers_deo_service_history_employeetypeid_idx ON kaveri.departmentusers_deo_service_history USING btree (employeetypeid);
CREATE INDEX departmentusers_deo_service_history_from_designationid_idx ON kaveri.departmentusers_deo_service_history USING btree (from_designationid);
CREATE INDEX departmentusers_deo_service_history_from_officeid_idx ON kaveri.departmentusers_deo_service_history USING btree (from_officeid);
CREATE INDEX departmentusers_deo_service_history_from_srocode_idx ON kaveri.departmentusers_deo_service_history USING btree (from_srocode);
CREATE INDEX departmentusers_deo_service_history_from_userid_idx ON kaveri.departmentusers_deo_service_history USING btree (from_userid);
CREATE INDEX departmentusers_deo_service_history_to_designationid_idx ON kaveri.departmentusers_deo_service_history USING btree (to_designationid);
CREATE INDEX departmentusers_deo_service_history_to_officeid_idx ON kaveri.departmentusers_deo_service_history USING btree (to_officeid);
CREATE INDEX departmentusers_deo_service_history_to_srocode_idx ON kaveri.departmentusers_deo_service_history USING btree (to_srocode);
CREATE INDEX departmentusers_deo_service_history_to_userid_idx ON kaveri.departmentusers_deo_service_history USING btree (to_userid);
